```q
(** ============================================================ *)
(** Auxiliary development for the strong normalization proof.    *)
(** ============================================================ *)

(** ** Substitution for free variables *)

Fixpoint subst (sigma : atom -> tm) (t : tm) : tm :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar i => tm_bvar i
  | tm_fvar x => sigma x
  | tm_app t1 t2 => tm_app (subst sigma t1) (subst sigma t2)
  | tm_abs T t1 => tm_abs T (subst sigma t1)
  | tm_true => tm_true
  | tm_false => tm_false
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => tm_if (subst sigma t1) (subst sigma t2) (subst sigma t3)
  end.

Definition ids (x : atom) : tm := tm_fvar x.

Definition env_lc (sigma : atom -> tm) : Prop :=
  forall x, locally_closed (sigma x).

Lemma subst_ids : forall t, subst ids t = t.
Proof. induction t; simpl; try reflexivity; congruence. Qed.

(** ** Free variables and freshness *)

Fixpoint fvs (t : tm) : list atom :=
  match t with
  | tm_bvar _ => (@nil atom)
  | tm_fvar x => (x :: nil)%list
  | tm_app t1 t2 => (fvs t1 ++ fvs t2)%list
  | tm_abs _ t1 => fvs t1
  | tm_true => (@nil atom)
  | tm_false => (@nil atom)
  | tm_if t1 t2 t3 => (fvs t1 ++ fvs t2 ++ fvs t3)%list
  end.

Fixpoint list_max (l : list atom) : atom :=
  match l with
  | nil => 0
  | x :: xs => Nat.max x (list_max xs)
  end.

Lemma list_max_upper : forall l x, In x l -> x <= list_max l.
Proof.
  induction l as [| y ys IH]; simpl; intros x Hin.
  - inversion Hin.
  - destruct Hin as [-> | Hin].
    + apply Nat.le_max_l.
    + eapply Nat.le_trans; [apply IH; eassumption | apply Nat.le_max_r].
Qed.

Lemma fresh : forall L : list atom, exists x, ~ In x L.
Proof.
  intros L. exists (S (list_max L)).
  intro Hin. apply list_max_upper in Hin.
  exact (Nat.nle_succ_diag_l (list_max L) Hin).
Qed.

Lemma fresh_two : forall L1 L2 : list atom, exists x, ~ In x L1 /\ ~ In x L2.
Proof.
  intros L1 L2. destruct (fresh (L1 ++ L2)) as [x Hx].
  exists x. split; intro Hin; apply Hx; apply in_or_app.
  - left; assumption.
  - right; assumption.
Qed.

(** ** Locally closed terms: inversion and closure properties *)

Lemma lc_app_inv : forall k t1 t2, lc_at k (tm_app t1 t2) -> lc_at k t1 /\ lc_at k t2.
Proof. intros k t1 t2 H. inversion H. split; assumption. Qed.

Lemma lc_abs_inv : forall k T t, lc_at k (tm_abs T t) -> lc_at (S k) t.
Proof. intros k T t H. inversion H. assumption. Qed.

Lemma lc_if_inv : forall k t1 t2 t3,
  lc_at k (tm_if t1 t2 t3) -> lc_at k t1 /\ lc_at k t2 /\ lc_at k t3.
Proof. intros k t1 t2 t3 H. inversion H. split; [assumption | split; assumption]. Qed.

Lemma lc_at_weaken : forall k k' t, lc_at k t -> k <= k' -> lc_at k' t.
Proof.
  intros k k' t H. revert k'. induction H; intros k' Hle.
  - apply lc_bvar. eapply Nat.lt_le_trans; eassumption.
  - apply lc_fvar.
  - apply lc_app; auto.
  - apply lc_abs. apply IHlc_at. apply Nat.succ_le_mono. assumption.
  - apply lc_true.
  - apply lc_false.
  - apply lc_if; auto.
Qed.
```

Hmm, the naming might be `IHH` or similar. Using `; auto` handles it if the IHs are in the form needed by auto ✓. Theorem fundamental : forall Gamma t T,
  has_type Gamma t T ->
  locally_closed t ->
  forall sigma, env_lc sigma -> env_sem Gamma sigma -> RR T (subst sigma t).

- T_Var: no need for Hlc ✓.
- T_Abs: Hlc : locally_closed (tm_abs T1 t1) → lc_at 1 t1 via lc_abs_inv.
  IH : forall x, ~ In x L -> P (update Gamma x T1) (open t1 (fvar x)) T2 = forall x, ~In x L -> locally_closed (open t1 (fvar x)) -> forall sigma, env_lc sigma -> env_sem (update Gamma x T1) sigma -> RR T2 (subst sigma (open t1 (fvar x))).
  Hmm, will Coq place `locally_closed (open t1 (fvar x))` inside the IH correctly? The motive P Gamma t T = lc t -> ...; applied to (update Gamma x T1) (open t1 (fvar x)) T2 gives lc (open t1 (fvar x)) -> ... ✓. And under the forall x, ~ In x L binder ✓. So IH x HxL : lc (open t1 (fvar x)) -> forall sigma, env_lc sigma -> env_sem ... ✓.
  
  Hbeta: for v with Hv : RR T1 v:
    x fresh (∉ L, ∉ fvs t1).
    lc (open t1 (fvar x)): from lc_at 1 t1 (from Hlc) and lc (fvar x) via lc_open_rec ✓.
    sigma' := env_update sigma x v; env_lc sigma' ✓ (Henv for y ≠ x, RR_lc Hv for x).
    env_sem (update Gamma x T1) sigma' ✓.
    IH x HxL (lc_open...) sigma' env_lc' env_sem' : RR T2 (subst sigma' (open t1 (fvar x))).
    rewrite with subst_open_rec (needs env_lc sigma' ✓) → RR T2 (open_rec 0 (subst sigma' (fvar x)) (subst sigma' t1)) → subst sigma' (fvar x) = v ✓, subst sigma' t1 = subst sigma t1 (x ∉ fvs t1) ✓ → RR T2 (open (subst sigma t1) v) ✓.

```q
Theorem normalization : forall t T,
  <{ empty |-- t \in T }> ->
  strongly_normalizing t.
Proof.
  intros t T H.
  assert (HR : RR T (subst ids t)).
  { eapply fundamental.
    - exact H.
    - apply has_type_lc. exact H.
    - intros x. apply lc_fvar.
    - intros x T' Hx. unfold empty in Hx. discriminate. }
  rewrite subst_ids in HR.
  apply RR_SN with (T := T). exact HR.
Qed.
```

Also, for the RR definition with lc:
```q
Fixpoint RR (T : ty) (t : tm) : Prop :=
  match T with
  | Ty_Bool => strongly_normalizing t /\ locally_closed t
  | Ty_Arrow T1 T2 =>
      strongly_normalizing t /\ locally_closed t /\
      (forall (T0 : ty) (b : tm),
          t -->* tm_abs T0 b ->
          forall u : tm, RR T1 u -> RR T2 (open b u))
  end.
```

And RR_SN, RR_lc:
```q
Lemma RR_SN : forall T t, RR T t -> strongly_normalizing t.
Proof. intros T t H. destruct T; simpl in H; tauto. Qed.
Lemma RR_lc : forall T t, RR T t -> locally_closed t.
Proof. intros T t H. destruct T; simpl in H; tauto. Qed.
```
✓.

Hmm, that requires me to supply all cases as functions. Actually that's quite clean and gives explicit names! Let me check the induction principle's shape:

has_type_ind : forall P : context -> tm -> ty -> Prop,
  (forall (Gamma : context) (x : atom) (T : ty), Gamma x = Some T -> P Gamma (tm_fvar x) T) ->
  (forall (L : list atom) (Gamma : context) (T1 T2 : ty) (t1 : tm),
      (forall x : atom, ~ In x L -> P (update Gamma x T1) (open t1 (tm_fvar x)) T2) ->
      P Gamma (tm_abs T1 t1) (Ty_Arrow T1 T2)) ->
  (forall (Gamma : context) (t1 t2 : tm) (T1 T2 : ty), P Gamma t1 (Ty_Arrow T1 T2) -> P Gamma t2 T1 -> P Gamma (tm_app t1 t2) T2) ->
  (forall Gamma : context, P Gamma tm_true Ty_Bool) ->
  (forall Gamma : context, P Gamma tm_false Ty_Bool) ->
  (forall (Gamma : context) (t1 t2 t3 : tm) (T : ty), P Gamma t1 Ty_Bool -> P Gamma t2 T -> P Gamma t3 T -> P Gamma (tm_if t1 t2 t3) T) ->
  forall (Gamma : context) (t : tm) (T : ty), has_type Gamma t T -> P Gamma t T.
